# The consonant round's readout (#175)

Generated by `python -m training.aug_readout doc --spec consonant1` from `tools/tadabur/consonant_readout/`'s `look.json` and `report.json`. The bars are #174's pre-registration, frozen 2026-10-22 before any training of the round and read exactly as written. Candidate: C95 continued toward the base teacher; baseline: C95. Nothing here trains, sets a threshold or touches the sealed panel.

## 1. The look half: live or EMA

Tarteel v1's look half, 4530 clips. Rule: the candidate's weights with fewer Tarteel v1 look-half false flags per 1,000 graded words (shipped default); a tie goes to higher word agreement with the teacher, then to live; no control arm (#178): the choice binds the candidate only. **Chosen: ema.**

| weights | graded words | flagged | false flags per 1,000 | word agreement with the teacher (points) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| candidate_ema | 43777 | 2002 | 45.73 | 66.70 |
| candidate_live | 43737 | 2028 | 46.37 | 66.46 |

## 7. Coverage of the frozen populations

The verdict and every table above apply to the reference-evaluable population recorded here. The excluded segments were not graded, so this readout does not measure them and does not bound their possible grades; the exclusions are reference-dependent, common to every model and not a random additional subsample. In particular the QUD guard's pass describes the evaluated share of the frozen half, not the whole pre-registered half.

| population | decoded | evaluated | excluded | excluded canonical word occurrences |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| qdat | 1505 | 1505 | 0 | 0 |
| qdat_bench | 159 | 159 | 0 | 0 |
| qud_half | 10423 | 10283 | 140 | 1168 |
| qud_other | 10261 | 10085 | 176 | 1503 |
| ship_a_verified | 8 | 8 | 0 | 0 |
| tadabur_dev_unseen | 671 | 671 | 0 | 0 |
| tarteel_v1.confirm | 4413 | 4408 | 5 | 21 |
| tarteel_v1.look | 4537 | 4530 | 7 | 22 |

**qdat**, the 0 excluded: no reference exclusion: every qdat clip has its corpus reference.

**qdat_bench**, the 0 excluded: no reference exclusion: the qdat clips the expert bench marks.

**qud_half**, the 140 excluded: the QUD pilot left these segments out as phonetizer-unsupported (its waqf/leen reference limitation), recorded in `qud_eval/pilot/report.json`; every model shares the exclusion.

**qud_other**, the 176 excluded: the QUD pilot left these segments out as phonetizer-unsupported (its waqf/leen reference limitation, `qud_eval/pilot/report.json`); every model shares the exclusion.

**ship_a_verified**, the 0 excluded: no reference exclusion: Ship A's held-out sites after the shared exclusions.

**tadabur_dev_unseen**, the 0 excluded: no reference exclusion: the dev clips h448 holds no copy of, read at stream_b0.

**tarteel_v1.confirm**, the 5 excluded: the phonetizer could not build a reference for these clips (`training.nonpro_eval.build_items` returns them unsupported); every model shares the exclusion.

**tarteel_v1.look**, the 7 excluded: the phonetizer could not build a reference for these clips (`training.nonpro_eval.build_items` returns them unsupported); every model shares the exclusion.

**Not run:**

- control: not run — no control arm (#178): the round's ingredient is more of C95's own recipe; #174 makes the control optional and non-gating.
- retasy: not run — RetaSy is not run: #171's baseline decode is not reproducible yet, and #174 reads it as descriptive only.

**Reported, not gating:** every substitution's change on the confirm half (§3), the false flags by cause (§2), `h448` and the teacher columns in every table, the catch side (§5), abstentions and coverage (§6–7), the #95 QUD half (§6), the teacher-equals subset beside bar 4b (§2), and G4's QUD-other-half companion (§2). RetaSy is #171's, absent here.
